Greater variation makes the robot more compliant to facilitate human guidance, whereas lower variation provides firmer assistance. In a real-world collaborative transport task with four possible directions, the proposed method achieved an average success rate of 0.95, compared with 0.83 for a fixed-stiffness ablation and 0.69 for a deterministic baseline. Near direction determination, variation among the sampled action chunks increased and the controller accordingly reduced stiffness. These results suggest that variation among actions sampled by a generative policy can serve as an online control signal for balancing assistance and compliance in physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [101] [RAEM: Robust Autonomous Exploration for Multi-Floor Environments with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25366)
*Zikang Yuan,Yuan Ren,Yian Wang,Yixue Wang,Enze Fang,Xuewei Zhang,Junda Cheng,Chi Chen,Chin-Pang Ho,Lijun Zhu,Shaohang Xu,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEM，一个面向四足机器人在多层建筑环境中进行鲁棒自主探索的框架。它采用混合局部-全局可通行性表示方法，结合局部断层扫描地图和显式分类的局部3D网格地图进行在线地形分析和连通性评估，同时增量构建具有高程感知的全局拓扑图以实现高效的跨楼层探索规划。实验表明该方法在多层结构（包括五层楼梯间连续探索）中表现出鲁棒且计算稳定的自主探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人探索方法依赖平面可通行性表示，无法充分表达多层建筑的叠层结构和跨楼层连通性；虽然基于断层扫描的表示方法能有效建模多楼层导航，但维护全局断层扫描地图在频繁重规划的在线探索中计算开销过大；此外，楼梯间稀疏且碎片化的LiDAR观测会降低局部可通行性估计质量，导致视点放置不规则和临时拓扑断连。

Method: RAEM采用混合局部-全局可通行性表示：局部使用断层扫描地图和显式分类的局部3D网格地图进行在线地形分析与连通性评估，全局则从这些局部空间表示中增量构建高程感知的全局拓扑图用于跨楼层探索规划。此外引入楼梯中心对齐策略以减少攀爬过程中的剧烈偏航变化，并提出双路径搜索机制以在全局拓扑局部断开时恢复引导路径。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验证明，RAEM在多层结构中表现出鲁棒且计算稳定的自主探索能力，包括在五层楼梯间进行连续探索。

Conclusion: RAEM通过混合局部-全局表示和针对楼梯场景的专门策略，有效解决了四足机器人在多楼层环境中的探索难题，在计算效率和探索鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RAEM, a robust autonomous exploration framework for quadruped robots operating in multi-floor environments. Most existing ground-robot exploration approaches rely on planar traversability representations, which cannot adequately represent the overlapping structures and cross-floor connectivity of multi-floor buildings. Although tomography-based representations provide effective traversability modeling for multi-floor navigation, maintaining a global tomography map incurs substantial computational overhead for online exploration with frequent replanning. Moreover, sparse and fragmented LiDAR observations in stairwells can degrade local traversability estimation, leading to irregular viewpoint placement and temporary topological disconnections. To address these challenges, RAEM adopts a hybrid local-global traversability representation, in which a local tomography map and an explicitly categorized local 3D grid map are used for online terrain analysis and connectivity evaluation, while an elevation-aware global topological graph is incrementally constructed from these local spatial representations for efficient cross-floor exploration planning. We further introduce a staircase center alignment strategy to reduce abrupt yaw variations during climbing and a dual path searching mechanism to recover guidance paths when the global topology is locally disconnected. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate robust and computationally stable autonomous exploration across multi-floor structures, including continuous exploration of a five-floor stairwell.

</details>


### [102] [A Taxonomy of Construction Task Activities for Robot Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25395)
*Sadman Sakib,Zhangyi None Peng,Yujie Pang,Yu Otsuki,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TARCAT，一个基于职业的施工机器人动作分类体系，从91项O*NET任务和30个教学视频中提炼出41个动作原语（分12组、3类），并支持将参数化原语序列组合为可复用技能，为施工场景中人形机器人的开发提供统一词汇表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型虽能赋予机器人更广泛的能力，但在施工部署场景中，需要精确掌握工人活动清单及其所需能力；缺乏一个系统化、可解释的分类体系来组织演示数据、明确机器人需求并支持技能库的搭建。

Method: 从7个高就业施工职业的91项O*NET任务和30个物理工作教学视频中提取分类信息，定义41个动作原语（12组、3类），并提供将参数化原语序列组合为可复用技能的机制；在DOBOT CR3机械臂和CRAFT手上演示了部分原语。

Result: 构建了TARCAT分类体系，具备人类可解释结构，可用于组织演示、指定机器人需求、支持编码代理检索和扩展技能库；部分动作原语已在真实机械臂上完成演示验证。

Conclusion: TARCAT为分析人类工作与发展通用施工机器人提供了公共词汇表，是连接工人活动清单与机器人能力实现的桥梁。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action models offer a path toward robots with broader repertoires than conventional task-specific systems. Construction deployment, however, requires a precise inventory of worker activities and the capabilities needed to execute them. We present TARCAT, an occupation-grounded taxonomy derived from 91 O*NET tasks across seven high-employment construction occupations and 30 instructional videos of physical work. TARCAT defines 41 action primitives in 12 groups and three classes and provides a mechanism for composing parameterized primitive sequences into reusable skills. This human-interpretable structure can organize demonstrations, specify robot requirements, and support coding agents that retrieve and extend skill libraries. We also demonstrate selected primitives on a DOBOT CR3 arm with a CRAFT hand. TARCAT thereby provides a common vocabulary for analyzing human work and developing general-purpose construction robots. Annotations are available at https://github.com/AICPS/TARCAT-Taxonomy.

</details>


### [103] [LAC: Linear and Angular Compliance for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25405)
*Yang Liu,Zhongkai Gu,Wei Zhu,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAC是一个全身控制器，能同时实现对上半身施加力/力矩的线性和角度顺从响应，通过大规模增强数据集配合师生强化学习训练单一策略，在仿真和真实实验中验证了全身顺从行为和遥操作定位操作任务的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的类人机器人任务涉及与物体和人的物理交互，但现有控制器要么将外力视为扰动拒绝，要么仅限制在少数身体链节的顺从性且忽略角度效应，缺乏一个通用的、全身范围的、同时兼顾线性与角度顺从的控制器。

Method: 首先，将全身顺从响应合成为大规模增强数据集，利用人类交互数据提取接触帧并施加采样力与力偶事件；在每个接触链节上，外力与被动屈服运动学链产生的虚拟力矩共同驱动虚拟导纳（在指定刚度下）；随后通过师生强化学习训练单一策略来跟踪外部力/力矩下的顺从运动。

Result: 大量仿真与真实实验证明：上半身受外力/力矩时产生全身顺从响应；两种刚度命令在整个范围内均可单调调制；并适用于遥操作定位操作任务。

Conclusion: LAC提供了一种通用的全身顺从控制器，同时实现线性与角度顺从，为类人机器人在与人/物交互的真实任务中提供了更灵活、可调谐的物理交互能力。

Abstract: Real-world humanoid tasks involve physical interaction with objects and humans, yet current controllers either reject external forces as disturbances or restrict compliance to limited body links while ignoring angular effects. We present LAC, a general whole-body controller that simultaneously realizes commanded Linear and Angular Compliance for wrenches applied to the upper body. First, we synthesize whole-body compliant responses into a large-scale augmented dataset. Sampled force and couple events are imposed on contact frames extracted from human interaction data. At each contact link, the external force and a virtual torque from the passively yielding kinematic chain drive a virtual admittance under the commanded stiffness. Subsequently, teacher-student reinforcement learning trains a single policy to track the compliant motions under external wrenches. Finally, extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate whole-body compliant responses to wrenches across the upper body, monotonic modulation over the full range of both stiffness commands, and applicability to teleoperated loco-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://lac-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [104] [SUPER ODOMETRY 2.0: Resilient Odometry via Hierarchical Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25427)
*Shibo Zhao,Sifan Zhou,Yuchen Zhang,Ji Zhang,Chen Wang,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Super Odometry，一种能够动态适应不同环境退化程度的传感器融合框架，通过四级自适应层次结构（特征选择、状态方向选择、引擎选择和基于学习的惯性里程计）将IMU提升到与相机和LiDAR同等重要地位，在超过200公里和800小时的多平台机器人实验中验证了其在恶劣环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有里程计系统在烟雾、沙尘暴、暴雪或低光照等严重传感器退化及极端条件下往往失效，威胁自动驾驶系统的安全性与功能性，亟需一种能在动态复杂环境中保持鲁棒性的里程计方案。

Method: 采用层次化传感器融合框架，从低到高集成四个核心自适应模块：自适应特征选择、自适应状态方向选择、自适应引擎选择，以及基于100多小时异构机器人平台数据训练的创新型学习式惯性里程计，将IMU提升到与相机和LiDAR同等重要的地位作为外部传感器失效时的可靠后备。

Result: 在覆盖空中、轮式和足式机器人平台的200公里里程和800小时运行中验证了方法的有效性，涵盖多种传感器配置、环境退化场景和激进运动模式。

Conclusion: Super Odometry为全退化环境下安全、长期的机器人自主性迈出了重要一步，证明了层次化自适应融合框架在实际复杂环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Resilient and robust odometry is crucial for autonomous systems operating in complex and dynamic environments. Existing odometry systems often struggle with severe sensory degradations and extreme conditions such as smoke, sandstorms, snow, or low-light conditions, threatening both the safety and functionality of robots. To address these challenges, we present Super Odometry, a sensor fusion framework that dynamically adapts to varying levels of environmental degradation. Super Odometry employs a hierarchical structure to integrate four core modules from lower-level to higher-level adaptability including adaptive feature selection, adaptive state direction selection, adaptive engine selection, and a novel learning- based inertial odometry. The inertial odometry, trained on over 100 hours of heterogeneous robotic platforms, captures comprehensive motion dynamics. Super Odometry elevates the inertial measurement unit (IMU) to equal importance with camera and LiDAR within the sensor fusion framework, providing a reliable fallback when exteroceptive sensors fail. Super Odometry has been validated across 200 kilometers and 800 operational hours on a fleet of aerial, wheeled, and legged robots, under diverse sensor configurations, environmental degradation, and aggressive motion profiles. It marks an important step towards safe and long-term robotic autonomy in all-degraded environments.

</details>


### [105] [Towards safe and optimal flight: Viability Kernel MPC for Fully Actuated Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25459)
*Massimiliano Bertoni,Alberto Piccina,Gianni Lunardi,Elias Fontanari,Andrea Del Prete,Angelo Cenedese,Giulia Michieletto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在模型预测控制（MPC）框架下，结合生存性理论与数据驱动方法，为全驱动多旋翼生成安全位姿轨迹的方法。通过动态计算轴对齐包围盒来保证避障安全性，无需离线可达性分析，并在全驱动倾斜六旋翼仿真中验证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业空中机器人需要在非结构化环境中导航时同时保证安全性、优化性能和计算效率，现有方法在形式化安全保证与实际计算开销之间存在矛盾。

Method: 在MPC框架中结合生存性（viability）理论与数据驱动方法，通过动态计算的轴对齐包围盒（AABB）强制执行避障约束，提供形式化安全保证，避免耗时的离线可达性分析。

Result: 在全驱动倾斜六旋翼上的数值仿真验证了该方法能够在杂乱环境中成功导航，并达到实时计算性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证安全性的同时实现了实时计算效率，为工业级全驱动多旋翼在非结构化环境下的自主导航提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial aerial robotics demands safety guarantees for navigation in unstructured environments while optimizing performance and computational efficiency. This paper presents a method for generating safe pose trajectories for fully actuated multirotors within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, leveraging both viability theory and data-driven methods. Obstacle avoidance is enforced through dynamically computed axis-aligned bounding boxes, providing formal safety guarantees without exhaustive offline reachability analysis. Numerical simulations on a fully actuated tilted hexarotor validate the approach, demonstrating successful navigation in cluttered environments with real-time computational performance.

</details>


### [106] [Transient multimode heat transfer of an industrial automated tape laying process under rapidly changing conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25470)
*Bernhard Rameder,Hubert Gattringer,Andreas Müller,Ronald Naderer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种工业自动铺带（ATL）过程的瞬态热传递模型，通过解析视角因子修正辐射热流的高估问题，并结合混合对流评估和单片求解策略，在工业线上实现了1.08%的NRMSE偏差，为热状态估计和原位固结提供了高保真物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热模型在复合材料制造中（尤其是ATL过程）受限于1.5D简化带来的辐射热流系统性高估，以及无法精确表征快速速度和电流调制下的热动态，难以支撑高质量的原位固结控制，因此需要一种更精确的瞬态热传递模型。

Method: 构建耦合平流、导热、对流和辐射的热传导方程；采用解析视角因子法处理有限发射器和带材宽度；通过理查德森数表征混合对流效应；将ATL系统分解为移动带材基底与红外热源两个子系统，带材采用双节点模型解析加热面与监测面之间的物理相位差；采用高阶隐式积分方案的单片求解策略保证高动态下的数值稳定性。

Result: 模型在工业ATL生产线上得到验证，在快速速度和电流调制条件下整体NRMSE偏差仅为1.08%。

Conclusion: 该框架为热状态估计提供了高保真、基于物理的解决方案，能够支持一致的现场固结并改善制件质量。

Abstract: This work presents a transient heat-transfer model of an industrial automated tape laying (ATL) process designed to overcome the limitations of conventional thermal models in composite manufacturing. The model solves the heat-conduction equation with coupled advection, conduction, convection, and radiation. A key innovation is the implementation of an analytical view factor approach that accounts for finite emitter and tape widths, thereby correcting systematic overestimations of radiative heat flux inherent in 1.5D simplifications. Furthermore, a local convection assessment incorporates mixed convection effects characterized by the Richardson number, ensuring accuracy across a wide range of process speeds. The ATL system is represented by two interacting subsystems: the moving tape substrate and the infrared heat sources. The tape is discretized using a two-node model that resolves the physical phase shift between the heated and monitored surfaces. Numerical stability under high dynamics is ensured by a monolithic solution strategy using a high-order implicit integration scheme. Model predictions were validated on an industrial ATL line, demonstrating an overall deviation of only 1.08% (NRMSE) under rapid velocity and current modulations. This framework provides a high-fidelity, physics-based foundation for thermal state estimation, supporting consistent in-situ consolidation and improved part quality.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamic Modeling of a Welding Torch Umbilical and Its Impact on Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25509)
*Nicolas Gautier,Yves Guillermit,Mathieu Porez,Fabien Rousset,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种焊接脐带缆的约束多体动力学模型，将其表示为一系列通过具有弹性和耗散效应的被动关节连接的刚体链，并通过全约束运动学约束引入远端锚点的预设运动。通过将动力学投影到允许速度子空间来消去拉格朗日乘子，提高了计算效率，并显式恢复了脐带缆对机器人施加的反作用力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着轻量化和协作机器人的广泛应用，外部脐带缆（焊接电缆）对机器人运动和驱动力产生的动态影响变得显著，有必要建立能够准确描述这种影响的动力学模型。

Method: 将焊接脐带缆建模为一系列由被动关节（含弹性与耗散效应）连接的刚体串链；通过全约束运动学约束施加远端锚点的预设运动；将动力学方程投影到允许速度子空间以消去拉格朗日乘子，得到无乘子的高效公式，并显式恢复作用在机器人上的反作用力螺旋。

Result: 提出了高效的约束多体动力学公式，避免了拉格朗日乘子的计算，并通过平面案例研究对该方法进行了验证说明。

Conclusion: 该方法为分析和补偿脐带缆对焊接机器人动态性能的影响提供了有效的建模工具，并通过平面案例验证了其可行性与计算效率。

Abstract: Robotic welding is widely used in industrial manufacturing, where the welding torch is often connected to the generator through an external umbilical. With the increasing deployment of lightweight and collaborative robots, the dynamic influence of this umbilical can significantly affect the robot motion and the actuation forces. This paper proposes a constrained multibody dynamic model of a welding umbilical, represented as a serial chain of rigid bodies interconnected by passive joints with elastic and dissipative effects. Prescribed motions at the distal anchor point are introduced through holonomic kinematic constraints. The equations of motion are reduced by projecting the dynamics onto the subspace of admissible velocities, yielding an efficient formulation free of Lagrange multipliers. The reaction wrench exerted by the umbilical on the robot is explicitly recovered. A planar case study illustrates the approach.

</details>


### [108] [A Tendon-Driven Five-Fingered Hand with Distributed Tactile Perception for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25547)
*Huayang Chen,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有分布式触觉感知的腱驱动五指灵巧手，采用软硬混合结构，兼具柔顺性与操作力，并通过双模态触觉传感元件实现静态接触与动态力变化的同步检测，多种操作实验验证了系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将具身智能技术应用于人形机器人进行复杂操作需要高灵巧度和强触觉感知能力的机械手，而现有设计在这两方面受限。

Method: 设计了腱驱动五指手，采用软硬混合结构以兼顾柔顺性与操作力；在五个手指的远端和中节指骨上分布式布置双模态触觉传感元件，可同时检测静态接触和动态力变化。

Result: 通过数数手势、指-拇捏取、物体抓取和抓瓶触觉记录等操作实验，验证了集成驱动-感知系统的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的腱驱动五指手成功集成了驱动与感知功能，为人形机器人的复杂操作应用提供了可行的灵巧手方案。

Abstract: To apply the techniques of embodied artificial intelligence to human-oid robots for complex manipulations, dexterous robotic hands are indispensable, which are restricted by the dexterity and tactile perception capability. In this work, we proposed a novel design of tendon-driven five-fingered hand with dis-tributed tactile perception. With a soft-rigid-hybrid structure employed, both compliance and operational force are endowed to the hand. Dual-modality tactile sensing elements are distributed on the distal and middle phalanges of all five fingers, enabling the simultaneous detection of static contact and dynamic force variations. Manipulation experiments, including counting gestures, finger-to-thumb pinching, object grasping, and bottle-grasp tactile recording, demonstrate the feasibility of the integrated actuation-perception system.

</details>


### [109] [ConfAL-WM: Confidence-Guided Active Learning for Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25572)
*Xiang Liu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfAL-WM，一种基于置信度引导的主动学习框架，用于对具身世界模型进行后训练优化。该框架在EVAC基础上附加轻量级置信度探针，在潜在空间预测密集置信度图，并通过任务级预筛、帧/补丁级加权增强来提升后训练效率和预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件的世界模型在遇到新任务和新场景分布时，误差往往集中在局部时空区域（如机器人手臂、被操作物体、接触区域和被遮挡物体），但现有的后训练方法缺乏对这种局部误差的针对性处理。

Method: 1) 在EVAC的UNet解码器特征上附加轻量级置信度探针，在潜在空间预测密集置信度图；2) 将置信度图聚合成任务级、帧级和补丁级分数；3) 先用目标域小规模数据重训置信度探针并预热EVAC，再进行任务级预筛分配采样预算，最后采用选中的数据重训，并可选帧级或补丁级加权增强。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0上的实验表明：置信度引导的数据选择提升了后训练效率；密集的帧级和补丁级加权相比基于标量奖励、进度和评判器打分的基线，进一步提升了预测质量和具身轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: ConfAL-WM通过置信度感知的主动学习有效解决了世界模型在分布偏移下局部时空误差集中的问题，验证了密集置信度信息在数据选择和训练加权中的价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have become an important foundation for embodied prediction, planning, and synthetic data generation, but their errors under new task and scene distributions are often concentrated in localized spatiotemporal regions such as robot arms, manipulated objects, contact areas, and occluded objects. This paper presents ConfAL-WM, a confidence-guided active learning framework for post-training embodied world models. Built upon EVAC, we attach a lightweight confidence probe to UNet decoder features and predict dense confidence maps in the latent space. These maps are aggregated into task-, frame-, and patch-level scores, enabling both efficient data selection and localized training enhancement. Our pipeline first retrains the confidence probe and warms up EVAC with a small subset of target-domain data, then performs task-level prescreening to allocate sampling budgets, and finally applies selected-data retraining with optional frame or patch weighted data enhancement. Experiments on RoboTwin2.0 show that confidence-guided selection improves post-training efficiency, while dense frame and patch weighting further enhances prediction quality and embodied trajectory consistency compared with scalar reward, progress, and judge-based scoring baselines. A quick visual overview of this work is available at https://ConfAL-WM.github.io.

</details>


### [110] [RA-VLA: Retrieval-Augmented VLA for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25585)
*Sanghwan Jang,Minjin Jeon,Minsoo Kim,Seongjin Choi,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-VLA框架，通过行为对齐的上下文检索与落地执行管线，解决VLA模型在新型任务分布下的脆弱性问题，实现无需训练的机器人操作适应，在LIBERO基准和真实UR5e环境中取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在泛化到新任务时表现出显著的脆弱性；虽然ICIL提供了无需训练的替代方案，但现有框架存在适应瓶颈——浅层检索机制和行为惯性（锚定在预训练先验上）阻碍了专家上下文到可执行动作的有效转化。

Method: 提出RA-VLA：检索增强的VLA框架，包含行为对齐的上下文检索模块和落地执行管线，在可扩展架构中强制对功能线索的忠实遵循，实现无缝任务适应的同时保持推理效率。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实UR5e机器人环境中，RA-VLA在成功率和计算效率方面均优于现有方法，验证了其作为无需训练机器人适应框架的有效性。

Conclusion: RA-VLA建立了鲁棒的无需训练机器人适应框架，通过克服检索浅层化和行为惯性两大瓶颈，为VLA模型在开放场景中的泛化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a versatile foundation for general robotic manipulation, yet they exhibit significant brittleness when confronted with novel task distributions. While In-Context Imitation Learning (ICIL) offers a training-free alternative, existing frameworks suffer from an adaptation bottleneck that hinders the effective translation of expert context to executable actions. This failure originates from superficial retrieval mechanisms and an inherent behavioral inertia that anchors the policy to its pre-trained priors. To address these limitations, we present RA-VLA, a retrieval-augmented VLA framework that integrates behavior-aligned context retrieval with a grounded execution pipeline. By enforcing faithful adherence to functional cues within a scalable architecture, RA-VLA facilitates seamless task adaptation while preserving inference efficiency. Our empirical evaluations across the LIBERO benchmark and a real-world UR5e environment demonstrate that RA-VLA achieves superior success rates and computational efficiency, establishing a robust framework for training-free robotic adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [Advantage-Driven Explicit Memory for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25610)
*Yeonsoo Park,Mattia Racca,Guillaume Bono,Steeven Janny,Gianluca Monaci,Tomi Silander,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种配备非参数化显式记忆的社交导航智能体，该记忆索引关键事件的历史步骤，使策略能够将部分行为外包给显式记忆，并通过在部署阶段持续收集数据来增强对分布外（OOD）情况的泛化能力。在模拟中训练后，该方法有效缓解了sim-to-real差距，并提升了对稀疏高成本失败（如碰撞）的记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人策略主要依赖参数化的模仿学习或强化学习方法，智能体的行为完全存储在策略的网络参数中，这对表示学习算法施加了沉重负担。作者希望引入非参数化显式记忆来减轻这一负担，并解决社交导航中稀疏高影响事件（如人类碰撞）难以被有效记住和利用的问题。

Method: 该方法提出将非参数化显式记忆集成到循环PPO架构中，显式索引导致关键事件的先前步骤。利用隐藏状态进行记忆检索以捕捉连续的时空动态，并通过RL智能体的优势信号来挖掘稀有高影响事件。训练在模拟环境中进行，结合了逼真渲染和非视觉人群模拟。

Result: 实验表明，该智能体在面对OOD社交行为时表现出鲁棒性，其保留稀疏高成本失败（如人类碰撞）的能力得到提升，并且通过基于真实数据进行部分决策，自然地减轻了sim-to-real差距。

Conclusion: 非参数化显式记忆机制为社交导航智能体提供了一种有效的持续学习途径，使策略能够将部分行为决策外包给记忆系统，从而在部署阶段不断改进，更好地适应分布外情况和真实世界的社交行为。

Abstract: Robot policies are predominantly learned with classical parametric variants of imitation learning or RL, where training stores the agent's behavior exclusively in the policy's network parameters, putting a heavy burden on the representation learning algorithm. We propose a new navigation agent equipped with non-parametric memory which explicitly indexes prior steps leading to critical events. The advantages are twofold: first, it allows the policy to outsource some of its behavior into an explicit memory; second, it encourages a form of continual learning by allowing an agent to collect data from its testing episodes during deployment and therefore to better generalize to OOD situations. In the context of social navigation, we show that this improves the agent's capability to retain sparse, high-cost failures, such as human collisions. If the policy is trained in simulation, this also naturally addresses the sim-to-real gap, partially, by basing some of the decision making on real data. We integrate the explicit memory into a recurrent PPO architecture and use hidden states for memory retrieval to capture continuous spatiotemporal dynamics. The goal of exploiting rare, high-impact events is achieved by leveraging the RL agent's advantage signals. We train our agent in simulation with a combination of photorealistic rendering and non-visual crowd simulation and show that the agent is robust with respect to OOD social behavior.

</details>


### [112] [Leveraging Inter-object Affordances for Efficient Planning in Contact-rich Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25641)
*Pouya P. Niaz,Justus Piater,Alejandro Agostini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出U-TAMP方法，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成对象间交互约束的抽象表示，以增强任务和运动规划（TAMP）在接触密集型任务中的适用性，在模拟厨房场景中实现了显著更高的规划成功率和快1-2个数量级的规划时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TAMP方法在真实世界应用中受限，因为它们假设简化的对象模型，忽略了对接触密集型任务成功执行至关重要的物理属性；且在运动规划中使用子符号推理导致规划时间大幅增加、成功率下降。

Method: 提出统一TAMP（U-TAMP）方法，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成对象间可供性（affordances）的抽象表示，用于表征接触密集型任务中对象间的物理交互约束（如抓取和支持约束），并将这些约束融入U-TAMP规划域，以处理具有不同物理属性的对象。

Result: 在模拟厨房桌面整理场景中进行实验，与原始U-TAMP和基于VLM的最先进规划器相比，该方法实现了显著更高的规划成功率，并将规划时间提升了一到两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过VLM生成对象中心化的约束抽象，结合U-TAMP框架，能够有效处理异构形状、尺寸和材料对象间的接触丰富交互任务，显著提升规划效率和成功率。

Abstract: Traditional task-and-motion planning (TAMP) approaches primarily focus on defining sequences of actions along with the necessary geometric and kinematic constraints to execute long-horizon tasks. However, their applicability in real-world settings is limited, as they typically assume simplified object models that overlook key physical properties critical for the successful execution of contact-rich tasks. Moreover, they often use sub-symbolic reasoning during motion planning, which drastically increases planning time and decreases overall success rates. We propose a method that leverages a TAMP approach, defining object-centric abstractions of execution constraints, called Unified TAMP (U-TAMP), to execute robotic tasks involving interactions among objects with heterogeneous shapes, sizes, and materials. Using a Vision-Language Model (VLM), we generate abstractions of inter-object affordances for characterizing physical interaction constraints between objects in contact-rich tasks, such as grasp and support constraints. These constraints are used to enrich the U-TAMP planning domain to deal with objects with variable physical properties. We perform experiments in simulated kitchen table organization scenarios and compare our results with those of the original U-TAMP, as well as a state-of-the-art VLM-based planner that leverages common sense knowledge of objects' affordances for plan generation. Our approach achieves significantly higher planning success rates and improves planning times by one to two orders of magnitude compared to other methods.

</details>


### [113] [EgoNav: Bridging Learned Waypoints and Geometry-Aware Local Control for Robust Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25642)
*Jing Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Hairong Qu,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav是一个用于图像目标导航的分层系统，通过语义分割的可通行区域生成候选路径点，并结合学习预测器进行几何安全性和方向一致性评分，再由自适应局部路径规划器执行，在模拟器和实体人形机器人上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轻量拓扑地图的图像目标导航系统中，学习得到的路径点预测器可能产生违反几何约束或偏离全局路径的目标，且现有系统要么缺乏局部规划器，要么使用无法适应狭小空间的固定参数。

Method: EgoNav采用分层架构：首先从语义分割的可通行区域生成候选路径点，然后将其与学习得到的路径点进行几何安全性、方向一致性和学习先验保真度三方面的评分，最后通过根据细化结果调节参数的自适应局部路径规划器来执行精细化的路径点。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器和实体人形机器人上的实验表明，EgoNav在成功率和路径效率方面均持续优于当代基线方法。

Conclusion: EgoNav证明了在保留学习预测器导航直觉的同时，通过语义分割和自适应局部规划的分层框架能够有效解决路径点不安全和参数固定的问题，为室内机器人图像目标导航提供了一种更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation using lightweight topological maps is a practical paradigm for indoor robot deployment: the map requires only geotagged images, and localization relies on visual matching rather than precise pose estimation. However, learned waypoint predictors can produce targets that violate geometric constraints or deviate from the global path. Executing these waypoints safely further requires a local planner capable of collision avoidance, yet existing systems either lack one or rely on fixed parameters that cannot adapt to confined spaces. To address these limitations while retaining the navigational intuition of the learned predictor, we present EgoNav, a hierarchical system that implements this idea by generating candidates from semantically segmented traversable regions and scoring them alongside the learned waypoint for geometric safety, directional coherence, and fidelity to the learned prior. An adaptive local path planner then executes the refined waypoint with parameters modulated based on the refinement outcome. Experiments in Habitat-sim and on a physical humanoid robot show that EgoNav consistently outperforms contemporary baselines in both success rate and path efficiency.

</details>


### [114] [GaussianDream++: Efficient 3D Gaussian World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25659)
*Yuqing Jiang,Zijian Zhang,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Junjie He,Lei Yang,Haifang Qing,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的、策略原生的VLA扩展方法，通过在世界状态令牌和世界预测令牌中注入3D监督信号，在训练时利用高斯重建与预测增强模型对3D结构和未来动态的理解，推理时去除所有额外组件仅保留20个世界令牌，在LIBERO和真实机器人任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略的动作模仿目标对度量3D结构和短时物理演化的监督较弱；几何增强方法只改善当前场景理解，预测方法多在RGB或隐空间建模未来且部署成本高，需要在保持高效推理的同时提供有效的3D监督。

Method: 该方法将World State Tokens和World Prediction Tokens直接插入VLA主干，配合仅训练时使用的World Representation Head，将令牌解码为共享高斯基元上的当前世界与未来预测表示；通过静态-动态因子分解保留持久结构并将残差运动聚焦于交互相关区域；推理时移除所有训练辅助组件，仅保留20个世界令牌。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.6%、LIBERO-Plus上达到87.8%的成功率，在Camera和Layout偏移下有明显提升；真实机器人实验中平均成功率从29.2%提升到52.5%（对比复现的π0.5），同时保持高效的闭环控制。

Conclusion: 训练时的高斯重建与未来预测能提供有效的3D监督信号，而紧凑的策略原生设计使得推理时几乎零额外开销，验证了"训练时丰富监督、推理时轻量部署"这一范式的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet action-imitation objectives provide only weak supervision for metric 3D structure and short-horizon physical evolution. Geometry-enhanced policies mainly improve current-scene grounding, whereas predictive policies often model future dynamics in RGB or latent spaces and may incur substantial deployment cost. GaussianDream demonstrates that training-time current Gaussian reconstruction and future Gaussian prediction provide effective 3D supervision, but its dense VGGT/TGE-based prefix jointly carries state, dynamics, and action-conditioning information. We present \textbf{\methodname}, a compact, policy-native extension that inserts \textbf{World State Tokens} and \textbf{World Prediction Tokens} directly into the VLA backbone. A training-only \textbf{World Representation Head} decodes these tokens into a Current World and coupled Future Prediction over shared Gaussian primitives, while static--dynamic factorization preserves persistent structure and focuses residual motion on interaction-relevant regions. At inference, the head, renderer, auxiliary objectives, and VGGT/TGE pathway are removed, leaving only 20 world tokens without online Gaussian decoding or rollout. \method achieves \textbf{98.6\%} on LIBERO and \textbf{87.8\%} on LIBERO-Plus, with clear gains under Camera and Layout shifts. Real-robot experiments further improve average success from 29.2\% to 52.5\% over reproduced $π_{0.5}$ while maintaining efficient closed-loop control.

</details>


### [115] [PRISM: Projection-Integrated Sampling-Based MPC with Bayesian Cost Tuning for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25666)
*Alinjar Dan,Iryna Hurova,Karl Kruusamäe,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个基于投影集成的采样型模型预测控制（MPC）框架，利用GPU加速物理模拟器作为在线世界模型，解决复杂接触环境下的双臂操作问题。其核心创新在于QP引导的控制采样策略，通过将采样轨迹投影到运动学可行域内，实现广泛而可行的双臂行为探索。在PerAct²任务的多个变体和真实UR5e机器人上验证了方法的鲁棒性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱且接触密集环境中的双臂操作仍具挑战性，需要协调运动生成、交互感知规划以及在紧运动学约束下的可靠执行。现有方法难以同时满足广泛的轨迹探索和运动学可行性要求。

Method: 1) 提出QP引导的控制采样策略，将采样的关节速度轨迹投影到满足位置、速度、加速度和加加速度约束及初始速度边界条件的运动集合上；2) 设计定制的ADMM/Bregman-splitting QP求解器，利用关节独立可分离性和可复用矩阵分解加速在线计算；3) 使用贝叶斯优化在离线阶段自动调节任务代价权重。

Result: 在障碍物约束的球体运输、托盘运输、立方体交接和抬箱等PerAct²任务变体上，PRISM相比代表性采样基线方法展现出更高的鲁棒性和任务成功率，同时保持实时或近实时执行；并成功实现了在双UR5e机械臂上的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: PRISM证实了基于物理的在线规划在接触密集的双臂操作中的实际潜力，展示了结合投影策略与采样MPC能有效提升复杂双臂任务的表现与可行性。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation in cluttered, contact-rich environments remains challenging because it requires coordinated motion generation, interaction-aware planning, and reliable execution under tight kinematic constraints. We present PRISM, a projection-integrated sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that uses a GPU-accelerated physics simulator as an online world model for complex dual-arm manipulation.
  The main algorithmic contribution is a QP-guided control sampling strategy that decouples trajectory exploration from kinematic feasibility. At each MPC step, sampled joint-velocity trajectories are projected onto the set of motions satisfying joint position, velocity, acceleration, and jerk bounds, together with an initial-velocity boundary condition, before rollout evaluation. This enables broad yet feasible exploration of coordinated bimanual behaviors. To support efficient online execution, we derive a custom ADMM/Bregman-splitting QP solver that exploits joint-wise separability and reusable matrix factorizations. We further use Bayesian optimization to tune task-cost weights offline, reducing manual parameter selection.
  We evaluate PRISM on challenging variants of PerAct$^{2}$ tasks, including obstacle-constrained ball transport, tray transport, cube handover, and box lifting. Experiments show improved robustness and task success relative to representative sampling-based baselines, while maintaining real-time or near-real-time execution. We also demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer on dual UR5e manipulators, highlighting the practical potential of physics-based online planning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation. Project details, including code and supplementary videos, are available at \href{https://sites.google.com/view/prismbimanual}{\texttt{https://sites.google.com/view/prismbimanual}}.

</details>


### [116] [Opportunities of Self Supervised Learning for GNSS: Evaluation of a Deep Learning-Enhanced PVT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25674)
*Thomas Barbero,Bertrand Ekambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习增强的PVT（位置、速度、时间）算法，通过联合预测距离修正和不确定性，并结合JEPA自监督预训练，在密集城区多路径干扰场景下显著提升定位精度，尤其对未见过的严苛城市条件表现优异，证明了未标注GNSS数据在提升泛化性能方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市区域中多路径干扰严重削弱GNSS（全球导航卫星系统）定位精度，传统PVT算法难以应对复杂城市场景，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出深度学习增强的PVT算法：1）采用有监督目标联合预测距离修正量及其不确定性；2）引入基于JEPA的自监督预训练阶段提升特征表示质量；3）在多样化驾驶场景中评估算法性能。

Result: 算法在多样化驾驶场景中显著提升PVT精度，特别是在未见过的严苛城市条件下表现突出，验证了自监督预训练和未标注数据对泛化性能的贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究证明利用未标注的GNSS数据通过自监督预训练可以有效提升深度学习PVT算法的泛化能力，为城市环境下的高精度定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work proposes a Deep Learning Enhanced PVT algorithm to mitigate multipath interference in dense urban areas. A supervised objective jointly predicts range corrections and uncertainty, while a JEPA-based self-supervised pretraining stage improves representation quality. The algorithm is evaluated over diverse driving scenarios, substantially improving PVT accuracy, particularly for unseen harsh urban conditions. These results highlight the potential of unlabelled GNSS data to improve generalization performance.

</details>


### [117] [LM-X: Explainable Action Modeling with Progress, Event, and Uncertainty Prediction for Generalist Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25757)
*Jin Lou,Jingxuan Zhu,Andong Chen,Xupeng Wang,Yuan Xu,Yuexuan Li,Xingdong Zhu,Zhijie Zhu,Yingwei Ji,Wenpeng Nie,Jingyi Li,Liangliang Chen,Jinyan Liu,Zhiqi Song,Jidong Zhang,Hongming Li,Yuchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LM-X，一种在任务、事件和运动三个尺度上组织预测的通用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。它在线发出三个显式监督信号（RTG、ETG、异方差动作流）直接条件化动作生成，使解释内嵌于控制过程。在20,000+小时真实机器人轨迹上训练后，LM-X在50个RoboTwin2.0随机困难任务中取得74.1%成功率（对比GR00T N1.7的55.4%），在7个真实机器人任务中达68.6%（对比50.7%），验证了显式多时间尺度预测状态可增强控制并暴露可解释的内部估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用VLA策略主要依赖短视的动作预测，单一动作目标被迫隐含吸收任务进度、中间意图和局部可靠性，而这些控制状态在执行中保持隐藏，导致任务-事件-运动三个时间尺度的信息耦合瓶颈，缺乏在线可解释性。

Method: 受生物感觉运动控制的功能原则启发，LM-X在任务、事件和运动三个尺度上组织预测，在线发射三个显式监督信号并直接条件化动作生成：(1) RTG（return-to-go）衡量可见任务进度；(2) ETG（event-to-go）识别下一个语义转换；(3) 异方差动作流通过传播方差估计局部可靠性。此外采用受控的五任务预训练门控来验证设计，再进行大规模训练。

Result: 预训练门控中完整模型比纯动作骨干提升16.0个成功率点，比最强单头变体提升10.8个点。在20,000+小时真实机器人轨迹（含1,000+小时失败策略回放）训练后，LM-X在50个RoboTwin2.0随机困难任务中达74.1% vs GR00T N1.7的55.4%；在7个真实机器人任务中达68.6% vs 50.7%。RTG能追踪语义进度，方差在犹豫和振荡控制期间上升。

Conclusion: 显式多时间尺度预测状态能够同时增强控制性能和暴露可解释的内部估计，说明将任务进度、事件转换和局部可靠性作为在线显式信号条件化动作生成是一种有效范式，解释应内嵌于控制而非事后生成。

Abstract: Generalist vision--language--action (VLA) policies learn long-horizon behavior mainly through short-horizon action prediction and reveal little beyond sampled commands. This creates two coupled bottlenecks: a single action target must implicitly absorb task progress, intermediate intent, and local reliability, while these control states remain hidden during execution. Inspired by functional principles of biological sensorimotor control, we introduce LM-X , which organizes prediction across task, event, and motor scales without claiming anatomical correspondence. Three explicitly supervised signals are emitted online and directly condition action generation: return-to-go (RTG) measures visible task progress, event-to-go (ETG) identifies the next semantic transition, and heteroscedastic action flow estimates local reliability through propagated variance. Explanation is therefore intrinsic to control rather than generated post hoc. Before a costly 20-day pretraining run on 64 NVIDIA B200 GPUs, a controlled five-task pretraining gate verifies the design: the complete model improves success by 16.0 points over the action-only backbone and by 10.8 points over the strongest single-head variant. We then train LM-X on more than 20,000 hours of real-robot trajectories, including over 1,000 hours of failed policy rollouts. LM-X achieves 74.1\% across 50 randomized-hard RoboTwin2.0 tasks versus 55.4\% for GR00T N1.7, and 68.6\% versus 50.7\% across seven real-robot tasks. RTG tracks semantic progress and visible regression, while variance rises during hesitation and oscillatory control. These results show that explicit multi-timescale predictive state can strengthen control while exposing interpretable internal estimates.

</details>


### [118] [TacForcing: Streaming Action Generation with Execution-Time Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25798)
*Jianbo Zhou,Boyuan Zhao,Yuzheng Zhang,Yiyang Chen,Wenxin Chen,Qiuyue Li,Xiangyang Gu,Yuhan Cao,Xiao Xia,Yanzhe Hu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacForcing，一种流式动作生成框架，将执行过程中的触觉反馈实时纳入动作生成，取代传统的独立高频反应控制器，在仿真和真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于块（chunk）的视觉-语言-动作模型在执行前一次性预测完整动作序列，导致触觉条件在执行过程中变得过时，无法适应动作时间范围内接触状态的显著变化；现有触觉反应方法通常依赖独立的分离式高频控制器，增加了架构和训练的复杂性。

Method: TacForcing采用流式动作生成框架，将标准动作专家替换为流式动作专家，使动作生成能够以执行过程中不断演变的触觉观测为条件；同时引入执行感知触觉注意力（EATA），将触觉条件限制在即将执行的动作上，减少触觉采集与动作执行之间的时间错配。

Result: 在六个模拟UniVTAC任务和三个真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，TacForcing分别实现了65%和69%的平均成功率，在两个设置中均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: TacForcing通过流式动作专家融合执行时触觉反馈，无需额外的高频反应控制器即可实现接触丰富操作中的触觉响应能力，验证了其在仿真和真实场景中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires adapting to contact states that can evolve substantially within an action horizon. However, chunk-based vision-language-action models predict complete action chunks from observations collected before execution, leaving tactile conditioning stale during execution. Existing tactile-reactive approaches typically rely on separate high-frequency controllers, which increase both architectural and training complexity. In this paper, we introduce TacForcing, a streaming action-generation framework that effectively incorporates execution-time tactile feedback. Instead of employing a separate reactive controller, TacForcing replaces the standard action expert with a streaming action expert to generate actions conditioned on the evolving tactile observations acquired during execution. TacForcing also introduces Execution-Aware Tactile Attention (EATA), which restricts tactile conditioning to actions nearing execution, thereby reducing the temporal mismatch between tactile acquisition and action execution. Across six simulated UniVTAC tasks and three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, TacForcing achieves average success rates of 65% and 69%, respectively, outperforming strong baselines in both settings.

</details>


### [119] [AGRO-Nav: Autonomous Graph-based Orchard Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25799)
*Ho Young Yun,Jaemin Yu,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AGRO-Nav，一种面向果园环境的自动化静态全局路径规划框架。它从SLAM点云中的树干簇拟合树行线，自动构建稀疏拓扑图，通过Dijkstra搜索、Theta*连接和B样条平滑生成全局路径。实验表明其平均行中心跟随误差仅0.08m，显著优于A*（0.31m）和Theta*（0.43m）基线，且规划速度快4~5倍，在Isaac Sim中鲁棒性也优于RANSAC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园是半结构化环境，平行树行形成天然驾驶走廊，但行间间距狭窄、枝叶茂密，导致传统几何无关的网格规划器容易偏离行中心，产生碰撞风险（树干或树冠接触）。现有方法缺乏自动化的全局规划框架，且依赖人工航点，无法适应果园的实际场景需求。

Method: AGRO-Nav框架包含三个核心步骤：（1）从SLAM点云中通过树干聚类拟合树行线，自动化构建包含行内和行间连接的稀疏拓扑图，无需人工航点；（2）在此拓扑图上使用Dijkstra算法搜索全局路线，并通过任意角Theta*算法将路径连接到起点和终点；（3）使用三次B样条对路径进行平滑处理，生成适合差速驱动和四轮转向平台的行进轨迹。

Result: 真实果园测试中，AGRO-Nav的平均行中心跟随误差约0.08m，远低于A*（0.31m）和Theta*（0.43m）基线，同时规划速度提升约4~5倍。在Isaac Sim仿真中，AGRO-Nav在所有基线的误差最低，且在树密度降至70%时仍保持稳定，而RANSAC中线基线在该条件下性能退化。生成的轨迹为直行行中心段加受控转弯，适用于差速驱动和四轮转向平台。

Conclusion: AGRO-Nav通过几何感知的拓扑图构建，有效解决了果园环境中网格规划器偏离行中心的问题，兼具规划精度和计算效率，且在部分树缺失等退化场景下具有较强鲁棒性，为果园自动化导航提供了实用的全局规划方案。

Abstract: Orchards form semi-structured environments in which parallel tree rows create natural driving corridors, yet narrow inter-row clearance and dense foliage lead geometry-agnostic grid planners to drift off the row center and risk trunk or canopy contact. We present AGRO-Nav, an automated framework for static graph-based global planning in orchards. From tree-row lines fitted to trunk clusters in a SLAM point cloud, it builds, without any manual waypoints, a sparse topological graph of intra- and inter-row connectivity; a global route is then found by Dijkstra search on this graph, connected to the start and goal by any-angle Theta* segments, and smoothed with a cubic B-spline. In real-orchard trials, AGRO-Nav follows the row center with a mean error of about 0.08 m, far below the A* (0.31 m) and Theta* (0.43 m) shortest-path baselines, while planning roughly four to five times faster. In Isaac Sim, it attains the lowest error among A*, Theta*, and a reproduced RANSAC midline baseline and remains stable as tree density drops to 70%, where the RANSAC baseline degrades. The resulting trajectories---straight row-centered segments joined by controlled turns---suit differential-drive and four-wheel-steering platforms.

</details>


### [120] [Anytime Global Tensor Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25830)
*Sai Coumar,An T. Le,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出全局张量运动规划（GTMP）的泛化版本，将相邻层边连接推广为任意黑盒局部规划器，并设计了两种任意时刻策略：固定预算随机重启的任意时刻GTMP（几乎必然覆盖所有同伦类）和增量预算知情扩展的AO-GTMP（收敛到最优代价）。理论证明采样图覆盖所有存在δ-清晰有界长度代表路径的端点固定同伦类，且增加每层采样可指数级降低漏检概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GTMP方法受限于特定局部规划器，难以处理复杂运动规划中的多样性需求和实时性约束，需要一种更通用的框架来统一不同类型的局部规划器，并保证同伦类覆盖与最优性收敛的理论性质。

Method: 将GTMP推广为支持任意黑盒局部规划器构建相邻层边；提出两种任意时刻策略：（1）固定预算下随机重启的GTMP，覆盖所有同伦类；（2）增量预算下的知情扩展AO-GTMP，收敛至最优代价；并给出理论证明支撑采样图的覆盖性与采样密集度对漏检概率的影响。

Result: 在操作（manipulation）基准上达到最先进性能；在2D导航中能生成一批拓扑多样的解，而知情基線方法仅集中在单一或少数同伦类上。

Conclusion: 泛化GTMP在保持理论保证（同伦类覆盖、最优性收敛）的同时，通过灵活接入任意局部规划器提升了实际性能与解多样性，为运动规划提供了一种通用且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Global Tensor Motion Planning (GTMP) solves motion planning with batched tensor operations over a layered multipartite graph. We generalize GTMP so that adjacent-layer edges are realized by any black-box local planner (e.g., linear interpolation, splines, sampling-based planning, trajectory optimization, or generative sampling). We provide two anytime policies on top of this generalization: Anytime GTMP with random restarts at a fixed budget, which covers every homotopy class almost surely, and AO-GTMP with informed expansion with growing budgets, which converges to the optimal cost. We prove that a single sampled graph covers every endpoint-fixed homotopy class admitting a \(δ\)-clear representative of bounded length. We also prove that additional samples per layer reduce the per-layer miss probability exponentially, whereas stronger local planners reduce the required layer count only sublinearly. On manipulation benchmarks the method matches state-of-the-art performance, and on 2D navigation it returns batches of topologically diverse solutions, while the informed baselines concentrate on one or two classes.

</details>


### [121] [MA-VLA: Multi-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Collaboration and Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25864)
*Zaibin Zhang,Junlan Xiao,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Changxing Xia,Heng Zhou,Talas Fu,Enshen Zhou,Ruimao Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-VLA，一种通过原子动作分配实现多臂协作的统一框架。它将协作行为分解为中层原子提示并分配给各个机械臂，引入Arm Shuffle训练策略以增强角色无关的指令跟随能力，从而支持多臂组合泛化。实验表明，在传统的VLA模型在未见协作模式下表现失败的情况下，MA-VLA在仿真和真实世界中均能持续成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常将语言视为单一全局指令，缺乏显式的机械臂特定行为分配与组合机制，导致无法泛化到训练中未见过的协作模式。多臂协作作为具身操作的核心能力，亟需一种能够显式分配和组合机械臂行为的框架。

Method: MA-VLA将协作行为分解为中层原子提示（atomic prompts）并分配给各机械臂，实现显式子目标规范和跨任务组合复用。同时引入Arm Shuffle技术，在训练时对观测、状态和分配提示进行排列，强制模型学习角色无关的指令跟随，支持重组为未见过的协作模式（即多臂组合泛化）。并构建了测试时协作模式在训练集中不存在的基准。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界评估中，传统的SOTA VLA模型在未见协作模式下大多失败，而MA-VLA能持续成功，验证了结构化的逐臂原子动作分配在实现多臂系统可扩展泛化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化的逐臂原子动作分配为多臂具身系统的泛化提供了一条实用的路径，代码、模型和数据已开源。

Abstract: Multi-arm collaboration is becoming a core capability in embodied manipulation. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models integrate perception, language, and control, but most represent language as a single global instruction and do not provide an explicit mechanism for assigning and composing arm-specific behaviors. This design limits transfer to collaboration patterns that differ from those observed during training. We present MA-VLA, a unified framework for multi-arm collaboration via atomic action assignment. MA-VLA decomposes cooperative behavior into mid-level atomic prompts and allocates them to individual arms, enabling explicit subgoal specification and compositional reuse across tasks. To reduce reliance on fixed execution roles, we introduce Arm Shuffle, a training-time permutation of the observation, state, and assigned atomic prompts for each arm. This permutation enforces role-agnostic instruction following and supports recomposition into unseen coordination patterns, which we term multi-arm compositional generalization. We also construct a benchmark in which test-time collaboration patterns are absent in training set. Across simulation and real-world evaluations, prior state-of-the-art VLAs largely fail under these unseen collaborations, while MA-VLA consistently succeeds. These results indicate that structured, per-arm atomic action assignment offers a practical route to scalable generalization in multi-arm embodied systems. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/zhangzaibin/future-robots

</details>


### [122] [VISTA: Visually Inferred Spatial ConTact Attention for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25872)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenlong Dong,Yan Huang,Xianglin Chen,Zijian Lin,Jiaqi Yin,Yushan Liu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA-Policy，一种利用视觉形变场（VDF）作为高维视觉-物理反馈的模仿学习范式，无需专用触觉传感器即可实现精细接触丰富的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉为中心的模仿学习在遮挡或细微交互场景下难以提供精确的接触状态反馈，而专用触觉/力传感器虽能提供丰富接触信息但增加了硬件复杂度、校准要求和部署成本。因此需要一种既无需额外硬件又能提供精确接触反馈的方案。

Method: VISTA-Policy包含三个核心组件：1) 物理感知编码引擎，用于实时解码视觉形变场（VDF）；2) 能量聚合去噪机制，隔离真实交互信号；3) 形变增强策略网络，通过增量夹爪动作实现精确闭环校正。

Result: 在跨尺度物体抓取、拧瓶盖和书法书写三项任务上的大量评估表明，VISTA-Policy优于强纯视觉基线3D Diffusion Policy和触觉基线，并展现出对未见物体尺度的显著分布外泛化能力和对动态扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VISTA-Policy为通用精细操作提供了一条耐用且成本效益高的路径，无需专用触觉传感器即可在非结构化环境中实现精确的接触丰富操作。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise interaction feedback. While vision-centric imitation learning is prevalent, external visual observations provide indirect and ambiguous cues about contact states, particularly under occlusion or subtle object--gripper interactions; dedicated tactile or force sensors can provide rich contact information but introduce additional hardware complexity, calibration requirements, and deployment costs. To bridge this gap, we propose VISTA-Policy, an imitation learning paradigm that utilizes the Visual Deformation Field (VDF), a 3D displacement representation of a compliant gripper, as high-dimensional visuo-physical feedback. The framework integrates: 1) a Physics-Aware Encoding Engine for real-time VDF decoding; 2) an Energy Aggregation Denoising Mechanism to isolate true interaction signals; and 3) a Deformation-Augmented Policy Network with incremental gripper actions for precise closed-loop correction. Extensive evaluations on Cross-Scale Object Grasping, Cap Unscrewing, and Calligraphy Writing demonstrate that VISTA-Policy outperforms the strong pure-vision baseline 3D Diffusion Policy and the tactile baseline. VISTA-Policy further demonstrates substantial out-of-distribution generalization to unseen object scales and robustness against dynamic disturbances, offering a durable and cost-effective route toward general-purpose fine-grained manipulation in unstructured environments. Project videos and supplementary materials are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/vista-policy.

</details>


### [123] [Low-Resolution Perception for Robotic Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25874)
*Giuseppe Fabio Preziosa,Federico Vignoni,Chiara Castellano,Marco Faroni,Andrea Maria Zanchettin,Paolo Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于机器人装箱的感知框架，通过低分辨率深度传感实现规模化感知。框架利用重建线索驱动下一视角选择，抓取证据更新每物体稳定性估计，共同决定何时采集和何时抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装箱任务中，高分辨率深度传感成本高昂且难以规模化应用。如何在低成本、低分辨率感知条件下实现可靠的感知与抓取，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: 提出一个综合框架：(i)重建线索用于驱动下一最佳视角(NBV)选择，避免冗余视角同时保留任务相关几何信息；(ii)抓取证据更新每个物体的稳定性估计，二者联合决定采集策略和抓取时机。通过两步验证：低分辨率下效用函数的消融研究，以及跨策略的端到端评估。

Result: 消融实验验证了效用函数在极低分辨率下的有效性；端到端评估表明低分辨率感知是机器人装箱中实用且可扩展的选择。

Conclusion: 低分辨率感知配合合理的NBV和稳定性估计策略，可作为机器人装箱中高成本高分辨率感知方案的可行替代，实现规模化部署。

Abstract: This work tackles the problem of scalable perception for robotic packing with low-cost, low-resolution depth sensing. We propose a framework where reconstruction cues drive next-view selection and grasp evidence updates a per-object stability estimate, jointly deciding what to acquire next and when to grasp. During the reconstruction, a low-resolution Next Best View (NBV) strategy explicitly avoids redundant views while preserving task-relevant geometry. We validate the approach in two steps: (i) an ablation study of the utility function under very low resolution, and (ii) a full end-to-end evaluation across policies, showing how low-resolution perception is a practical, scalable option for robotic packing.

</details>


### [124] [A Statistical Audit of Physical AI Benchmark Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25940)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含51个模型、12个物理AI基准的评估矩阵，发现基准之间存在冗余性，并通过贪心算法筛选出4个代表性基准，保留78.5%的效用，同时拟合了Bradley-Terry排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI模型在不同报告中使用不同的基准套件进行评估，导致模型-基准矩阵稀疏，且基准之间的关系未被系统度量，难以进行公平和一致的模型比较。

Method: 从51个基准和152个模型的注册表中，依据报告密度筛选出51个模型和12个基准，结合模型卡片、基准论文及官方协议下的自评结果构建评估矩阵；度量基准间共享信息以识别冗余；基于评分离散度与未解释方差的组合效用进行贪心基准筛选；对筛选后的基准子集拟合Bradley-Terry排名模型。

Result: 发现了基准之间的定量冗余证据；将两个替代基准对合并为单列后，51个模型中有22个在等权平均排名下移动了3个或更多名次；筛选出的4个基准子集保留了全部12个基准78.5%的效用。

Conclusion: 基准冗余会显著影响模型排名，所提出的贪心筛选与Bradley-Terry排名方法能在保留大部分评估效用的同时降低成本，且该方法仅需有足够重叠的基准级评分，具有通用性，不仅限于物理AI领域。

Abstract: Physical AI models are evaluated on suites of benchmarks that differ across model reports, leaving the model-by-benchmark matrix sparse and the relationship between benchmarks unmeasured. We construct a matrix of 51 models on 12 physical AI benchmarks, selected from a registry of 51 benchmarks and 152 models by reporting density, combining scores from model cards and benchmark papers with our own evaluation runs under each benchmark's official protocol. We measure how much information the benchmarks share and show quantitative evidence of Redundancy. Redundancy affects reported rankings: collapsing the two substitute pairs into single columns moves 22 of 51 models by three or more places under an equally weighted average. We then select benchmarks greedily under a utility combining score dispersion with variance not explained by the already-selected set, and obtain a four-benchmark subset retaining 78.5\% of the utility of all 12, on which we fit a Bradley--Terry ranking. The procedure requires only benchmark-level scores with sufficient overlap and is not specific to physical AI.

</details>


### [125] [DESCENT: Directed Edge Scene Encoding for Airport Surface Movement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26002)
*Alexander Prutsch,David Schinagl,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DESCENT，一种基于Transformer的架构，用于机场场面运动的轨迹预测。通过PRS上下文采样机制和检测Transformer解码器，在Amelia-10基准上显著优于现有方法，尤其在安全关键场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商业航空交通密度不断增加，提高地面运行安全性至关重要。运动预测在自动驾驶领域已被广泛研究，但将其应用于机场场面运动仍缺少探索，亟需一种能够处理异构动力学和严格拓扑约束的高效准确预测方法。

Method: 提出DESCENT架构：1）基于Transformer处理异构动力学与拓扑约束；2）设计潜在可达集（PRS）上下文采样机制，自适应收集不同运行阶段的机场环境上下文；3）结合检测Transformer解码器生成精确的轨迹预测。

Result: 在Amelia-10基准上的大量实验表明，DESCENT相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著的性能提升，特别是在安全关键场景中优势更加明显，域感知采样提供了安全导航所需的关键长时程上下文。

Conclusion: DESCENT通过领域自适应的上下文采样机制有效解决了机场场面运动预测中的异构动态和拓扑约束难题，在安全关键场景中展现出显著的预测优势，为机场地面运行安全提供了有力技术支持。

Abstract: Advanced automation is a key technology for enhancing the safety of ground operations amidst the increasing density of commercial air traffic. While motion forecasting is a well-studied task in autonomous driving, its application to airport surface movements remains underexplored. To enable efficient and accurate prediction in this domain, we propose DESCENT, a transformer-based architecture designed to handle heterogeneous dynamics and strict topological constraints. Our approach features a Potential Reachable Set (PRS) context sampling mechanism that adaptively collects airfield environment context across diverse operational phases. Combined with a detection transformer-based decoder, DESCENT generates accurate trajectory forecasts. Extensive evaluations on the Amelia-10 benchmark demonstrate significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. These gains are especially pronounced in safety-critical scenarios, where our domain-aware sampling provides critical long-horizon context necessary for safe navigation.

</details>


### [126] [Phantom Navigator: Stealthy and Precise Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Redirection with Real-Time Tracking and GPS Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26011)
*Haocheng Meng,Shaocheng Luo,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Phantom Navigator，一种通过LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪和欺骗技术，将无人机隐蔽且精确地重定向到指定目标的攻击方法，并通过真实世界案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机重定向攻击（物理拦截、通信劫持、传感器欺骗）在可靠性、精准性和隐蔽性方面存在不足：物理拦截成本高、机会单一且有附带损伤风险；网络攻击需要深度技术且依赖加密通信访问；传感器欺骗难以达到引导无人机到指定目标所需的精度和鲁棒性。

Method: Phantom Navigator采用两阶段方法：离线预重定向可达性分析提供高保真的重定向范围估计；在线闭环隐蔽执行层确保实际重定向成功。同时构建了配备LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪和欺骗堆栈的物理攻击平台，实现实时识别、位姿估计和定向欺骗信号计算。

Result: 通过真实世界案例研究验证了重定向方法和攻击实现的有效性，展示了该攻击能够隐蔽且精准地将受害者无人机重定向到指定位置。

Conclusion: Phantom Navigator提供了一种比现有方法更可靠、更精准且更隐蔽的无人机重定向攻击方案，实现了将目标无人机精确引导至指定欺骗位置的目标。

Abstract: Redirecting unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) from their intended mission trajectories has been an active area of research. However, existing UAV redirection attacks lack reliability, precision, and covertness for a targeted diversion. They primarily rely on physical capture, communication hijacking, or sensor spoofing. Yet, physical interception is costly, offers only a single opportunity for success, and poses a high risk of collateral damage; network-based attacks demand deep technical expertise and access to encrypted communication channels; and sensor spoofing techniques typically fall short in achieving the accuracy and robustness required to steer a UAV toward a specified target. Consequently, we propose Phantom Navigator, a UAV redirection attack to mislead drones to a designated spoofing target, covertly and precisely. Our approach combines offline pre-redirection reachability analysis, which provides high-fidelity estimates of achievable redirect ranges, with an online closed-loop, stealthy execution layer that ensures successful redirection in practice. Based on this approach, we build a physical attack platform equipped with a LiDAR--camera detection, tracking, and spoofing stack that performs real-time identification, pose estimation, and computation of targeted spoofing signals to covertly and accurately redirect victim UAVs to a designated location. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our redirection methodology and the attack implementation in real-world case studies.

</details>


### [127] [When Obstacles Bend: Modeling Vegetation Deformation in the context of Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26050)
*Muhammad Hsaeeb Zaar Khizar,Tom Montagnon,Roland Lenain,Romuald Aufrère,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种独立于具体机器人平台的植被机械特性表征方法，通过结合形变测量与接触力数据来估计植被的固有机械参数，从而实现基于环境内在属性的植被感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统穿越性评估从机器人视角出发，将环境评估与机器人自身动力学耦合，导致结果难以跨平台迁移，且无法直接反映植被本身的物理属性，而这恰恰是农业和环境监测等应用中交互与潜在损伤的真正来源。

Method: 将形变测量与接触力数据相结合，估计植被的底层机械参数，并重建植被与交互的响应关系，从而实现与具体机器人无关的植被特性表征。

Result: 该方法能够基于植被固有的机械属性进行植被感知导航，取代以往依赖于特定平台的穿越性度量。

Conclusion: 通过对植被固有机械性质的直接表征，可以脱离具体平台限制，为自主机器人在自然环境中进行植被交互提供更通用且物理意义上更准确的导航依据。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in natural environments must often interact with vegetation rather than simply avoid it. In this context, traversability is typically defined from the robot's perspective, by measuring how a specific platform responds when moving through the environment. While practical, this viewpoint entangles the assessment of the environment with the robot's own dynamics, making the resulting characterization difficult to transfer across different platforms. More importantly, it does not directly reflect the properties of the vegetation itself, which are the true source of interaction and potential damage in applications such as agriculture and environmental monitoring. To address this limitation, we propose to characterize vegetation through its intrinsic mechanical properties, independently of any specific robot. By combining deformation measurements with contact force data, we estimate the underlying mechanical parameters and reconstruct the vegetation's response to interaction. This enables vegetation-aware navigation based on intrinsic environmental properties rather than platform-dependent metrics.

</details>


### [128] [$R^3$: Training Robots to Reason in Natural Language via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26053)
*Lehong Wu,Yuxiao Qu,Zheyuan Hu,Ivan Zhang,Limin Wei,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出R³，一种简单的后训练方法，能将现成的VLM转变为机器人推理器：通过专家生成的推理轨迹进行中期训练，再利用基于规则的强化学习从离线动作数据中改进推理器，使VLM能用自由形式的自然语言推理来引导底层操作策略，在长时程操作任务中显著优于仅指令模仿学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言推理能让基础模型在困难问题上花费更多测试时计算，但该方法是否能改善机器人操作尚不清楚。长时程操作任务需要跟踪部分进度、推理物体关系、从错误中恢复以及引导嘈杂的低层策略，这些挑战促使研究者探索训练VLM直接在自然语言中推理来引导操作策略。

Method: R³后训练配方包含两步：首先在专家生成的推理轨迹上对VLM进行中期训练，以初始化期望的推理风格；然后利用基于规则的强化学习（rubric-based RL），从离线动作数据中单步改进推理器。与以往主要将结构化轨迹作为辅助监督的方法不同，R³训练自由形式的语言推理以产生测试时的动作引导。

Result: 在Language Table和模拟双臂杂货打包两个受控测试平台上，R³改善了探索能力和在未见任务上的泛化能力，在两个基准上均显著优于仅指令模仿学习基线。分析表明自由形式的语言推理可以作为测试时计算机制来引导低层策略。

Conclusion: R³验证了训练VLM进行自由形式语言推理以引导低层操作策略的可行性，表明语言推理可以作为一种测试时计算机制，为长时程机器人操作中的探索和泛化带来显著提升。

Abstract: Reasoning in language allows foundation models to spend more test-time compute on hard problems, such as those requiring decomposition, constraint tracking, and prediction of future consequences. Whether this mechanism can improve robotic manipulation remains unclear, where long-horizon tasks require tracking partial progress, reasoning about object relations, recovering from mistakes, and steering noisy low-level policies. In this paper, we study whether VLMs can be trained to reason directly in natural language to guide low-level manipulation policies. We introduce $R^3$, a simple post-training recipe that turns off-the-shelf VLMs into robotic reasoners: it first mid-trains a VLM on expert-generated reasoning traces to initialize the desired reasoning style, then improves the reasoner with single-step rubric-based RL from offline action data. Unlike prior robotic reasoning methods that mostly use structured traces as auxiliary supervision, $R^3$ trains free-form language reasoning to produce test-time guidance for action. We instantiate $R^3$ on Language Table and simulated bimanual grocery packing, two controlled testbeds for studying robotic reasoning and long-horizon manipulation. $R^3$ improves exploration and generalization across unseen tasks and significantly outperforms instruction-only imitation learning baselines on both benchmarks. Our analyses suggest that free-form language reasoning can function as a test-time compute mechanism for steering low-level policies. Our project page is available at https://robotic-reasoner.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [One Policy, Many Embodiments: Unified Camera-Centric Action Geometry Pre-training for Heterogeneous Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26058)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,University of Macau,:,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Guozhi Zhan,Zhixiang Duan,Yuhan Wang,Yuechen Luo,Shengyin Jiang,Hanbing Li,Zhiying Du,Longlong Wang,Longmei Jiang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yong Pan,Ziping Zhao,Zhiyuan Chen,Yangwei You,Kun Ma,Qinyuan Liu,Hangjun Ye,Zhi-xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UCAG-P提出了一种以相机为中心的统—动作表征框架，将异构具身数据在共享几何动作空间中对齐，使单一的VLA策略能够跨不同机器人形态、摄像头配置和动作空间进行联合学习，无需基准特定微调即可在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展通才VLA策略严重受限于具身数据的异构性（不同的机器人形态、摄像头配置、低层动作空间）。现有方法通过显式动作重定向、人机视频合成或数据集特定适配分支来解决，但本质上阻碍了统一策略的联合学习。

Method: UCAG-P提出以相机为中心的统一动作公式化方法：1) 将机器人手臂、人形机器人和人手统一为共同动作模式的不同具身形式；2) 通过可观测的锚点运动在图像和相机帧坐标中表征操作；3) 使用几何条件动作翻译器将预测运动与目标具身运动学结合生成可执行控制；4) 解耦架构允许共享VLA策略学习可迁移的操作几何，同时保留具身特定的可控性。

Result: 在4.03K小时机器人及仿真数据加上2.34K小时人类演示数据上训练。单一检查点在LIBERO上达到98.3%，RoboTwin Easy和Hard分别达到88.7%和89.2%，LIBERO-Plus零样本达到82.0%，RoboCasa GR-1达到62.0%，均无需基准特定微调。

Conclusion: UCAG-P通过结构性统一异构具身数据到共享几何动作空间，有效解耦了策略学习与执行控制，验证了以相机为中心的锚点运动表征可作为统一的跨具身策略学习范式，为通才VLA策略的可扩展性提供了新路径。

Abstract: Scaling generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies is severely bottlenecked by the inherent heterogeneity of embodied data, which spans diverse robot morphologies, camera configurations, and low-level action spaces. Existing paradigms typically address this mismatch through explicit action retargeting, human-to-robot video synthesis, or dataset-specific adaptation branches, fundamentally hindering the joint learning of a unified policy. We introduce UCAG-P, a camera-centric unified action formulation that structurally aligns heterogeneous embodied datasets into a shared geometric action space. Rather than treating robot-specific commands as the shared policy target, UCAG-P represents manipulation through camera-observable anchor motion in image and camera-frame coordinates, treating robot arms, humanoids, and human hands as different embodiments of a common action schema. A geometry-conditioned action translator combines predicted motion with target-embodiment kinematics to produce executable controls. The resulting decoupled architecture allows a shared VLA policy to learn transferable manipulation geometry while retaining embodiment-specific controllability. UCAG-P is trained on 4.03K hours of robot and simulation data and 2.34K hours of human demonstrations. A single checkpoint reaches 98.3% on LIBERO, 88.7% and 89.2% on RoboTwin Easy and Hard, 82.0% zero-shot on LIBERO-Plus, and 62.0% on RoboCasa GR-1, without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [130] [VirTooS: A ROS 2 - Unity Virtualization Toolkit for Fleet Management of Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26066)
*Andrea Drudi,Lorenzo Pichierri,Andrea Testa,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VirTooS是一个基于Python/C#的工具包，利用ROS 2和Unity游戏引擎，为自主移动机器人（AMR）编队提供混合现实环境下的可扩展虚拟实验平台，支持虚拟与真实传感器融合，并通过ChoiRbot框架实现分布式机器人实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人编队管理需要现实、可扩展的虚拟实验环境，以便在部署到真实场景前进行验证和测试。

Method: 采用ROS 2和Unity游戏引擎构建混合现实仿真环境，提供场景生成与定制功能，融合虚拟与真实传感器（如LiDAR）进行地图构建和安全导航，利用ChoiRbot框架实现分布式实验，并提供容器化套件便于跨机器部署。

Result: 通过任务分配问题的一系列实验展示了真实机器人与虚拟机器人在混合现实环境中无缝交互的能力。

Conclusion: VirTooS为AMR编队的舰队管理任务提供了实用且可部署的仿真工具包，源代码将公开在GitHub上供社区使用。

Abstract: In this paper, we present VirTooS, a Python/C# toolkit designed to implement fleet-management tasks on teams of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). VirTooS leverages the Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 and Unity game engine to provide realistic, scalable virtual experiments in a mixed-reality environment. The toolbox allows users to easily generate and customize virtual scenarios for realistic simulations. Virtual and real sensors as, e.g., LiDARs, can be exploited to map and safely navigate in the mixed-reality environment. To enable distributed robotics experiments, we propose a set of tailored routines leveraging the ChoiRbot framework. As a motivating example, we show a set of experiments for task assignment problems in a virtual environment, allowing seamless interaction among real and virtual robots. Moreover, the package comes with a containerized suite to easily deploy it on different machines. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [131] [Gating Before Commitment: Anticipating Intent Divergence to Prevent Post-Interaction Decision Failures in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26074)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个语言引导的意图模块驱动的决策门控层，用于在车辆交互中修复因意图误读导致的规划失败。在回放的越野偏移和四个碰撞片段上，该门控层是唯一能修复计划的层级，能将轨迹保持在走廊内。通过将评分不确定性视为弃权而非冲突，误触发率从每5.9分钟9次降至每0.341次/分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆交互过程中的意图误读会导致反复的规划失败，需要一种新的决策机制来在规划提交前修复这类错误。

Method: 在走廊包络上游部署一个语言引导的意图模块决策层：模块读取结构化描述符，计算平滑的意图-几何散度评分，并在提交前门控规划机动。通过预注册的重新设计将评分不确定性视为弃权（而非半冲突）来校准，并进行两次消融实验。

Result: 在主案例中，门控在漂移开始后72毫秒触发，但在走廊退出前161毫秒触发，10次重播均保持轨迹在走廊内。首次校准在5.9分钟内产生9次误触发，预注册重设计将其降至0.341次/分钟。完整评分在五个失败中的四个上检测最快；消除置信度项导致丢失两个检测。在相等误报率下，几何规则在领域内轨道上的检测率提高三倍以上。

Conclusion: 证据支持门控机制的有效性；该模型的主要贡献是对这些失败的快速检测以及对几何规则提供不确定性否决。

Abstract: Intent misinterpretation during vehicle interactions causes recurring planning failures. We study a decision layer in which a language-guided intent module reads structured descriptors, computes a smoothed intent-geometry divergence score, and gates the planned maneuver before commitment, upstream of a corridor envelope. On a replayed off-road departure and four crash clips under a frozen, disclosed implementation, gating is the only layer that repairs the plan: on the main case it fires 72 ms after the drift onset but 161 ms before the corridor exit, keeping the trajectory in the corridor in all ten replays. The first calibration draws nine false triggers in 5.9 minutes, each from scoring uncertainty as half a conflict; a preregistered redesign treating uncertainty as abstention cuts this to 0.341 per minute. Two ablations bound the model's contribution: the full score detects fastest on four of five failures under the deployed eligibility, three of five against the unvetoed rule (000871 by one cycle; 000228 by a pre-onset fire on an uncertain stretch that five clips cannot classify as signal or coincidence; dropping the confidence term costs two detections), while on in-domain tracks at equal false positives the geometric rule more than triples its detection. The evidence supports the gating mechanism; the model's demonstrated roles are the fastest detection on these failures and an uncertainty veto on the geometric rule.

</details>


### [132] [Fast Generative Grasping via Lie Group-Constrained MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26076)
*S. Talha Bukhari,Yi Wei,Ruiqi Ni,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于李群MeanFlow的快速生成式抓取合成方法，在产品李群SO(3)×ℝ³上结合代数半群一致性条件与黎曼条件流匹配进行训练，仅需≤5次网络评估即可生成可靠抓取，在ACRONYM数据集上达到与SOTA扩散/流模型相当的抓取性能，推理延迟达毫秒级（最高39倍加速），并能直接迁移到真实机器人抓取场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人抓取需要学习多模态分布，现有基于扩散和流的方法虽灵活泛化，但多步采样的迭代过程严重阻碍了机器人操作中时间敏感（time-critical）的应用需求，亟需在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低采样延迟。

Method: 提出Lie Group-constrained MeanFlow方法：将抓取建模为乘积李群G=SO(3)×ℝ³上的分布；训练目标将纯代数半群一致性条件与黎曼条件流匹配相结合，将平均速度锚定到数据分布；通过该约束实现极少的采样步数（≤5次网络评估）即可生成高质量抓取。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上，该方法以毫秒级推理延迟匹配了SOTA扩散和流模型的抓取生成性能，实现了最高39倍的推理加速；并验证了无需额外训练或域适应即可直接迁移到真实机器人抓取，在观测噪声下仍表现出鲁棒的抓取合成能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过将几何结构与代数一致性约束融入生成模型训练，可以在保持生成质量的同时实现极快的采样速度，为实时机器人抓取中部署生成式模型提供了有效路径，并展现了从仿真到真实场景的强迁移能力。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis is a core task in robotic manipulation, for which the solution typically forms a multimodal distribution rather than a point estimate. Generative robotic grasping aims to learn this distribution with deep generative models such as diffusion and flow-based approaches. The iterative nature of such generative models makes them flexible and generalizable; however, multi-step sampling impedes the time-critical operation required in robotics. We devise an approach to fast generative grasping based on MeanFlow on the product Lie group $\mathcal{G} = \mathrm{SO}(3) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. The training objective couples a purely algebraic semigroup consistency condition with Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching on $\mathcal{G}$ that anchors the average velocity to the data distribution. The resulting Lie Group-constrained MeanFlow formulation samples reliable grasps in $\leq 5$ network evaluations, matching the grasp generation performance of state-of-the-art diffusion and flow-based models on the ACRONYM dataset at millisecond-scale inference latency (up to $39\times$ speed-up). We further demonstrate that the approach directly translates to real-world robotic grasping without additional training or domain adaptation, exhibiting robust grasp synthesis under observation noise.

</details>


### [133] [Zero-WAM: In-Context World-Action Modeling from Human Videos for Open-Ended Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26103)
*Jiaming Zhou,Qihang Zhang,Gangwei Xu,Cunxin Fan,Yujie Zhao,Ruilin Wang,Yiming Luo,Shuai Yang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Junwei Liang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-WAM，一种将大语言模型中的上下文学习范式引入机器人操作领域的因果视频-动作模型。它通过人类视频作为任务规范输入，使策略能够执行训练中从未见过的操作任务。为解决人-机器人配对数据稀缺问题，作者构建了自动转换流程生成HumanGen数据集（74.2K配对样本，8.6K任务），并设计了上下文未来块预测目标来抑制捷径学习。在RoboTwin 2.0仿真七个未见任务上取得47.0%平均成功率，比最强基线绝对提升29.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本跨任务泛化是机器人学习的核心挑战。大语言模型通过上下文学习即可执行新任务而不需更新参数，受此启发，作者希望将这一范式引入机器人操作领域，以人类视频代替语言作为任务规范，从而实现机器人对未见操作任务的泛化。

Method: 提出了Zero-WAM，一个因果视频-动作模型，通过上下文人类视频引导执行未见任务。主要方法包括：(1) 自动流水线将任务采样的机器人轨迹转换为语义匹配的人类视频，构建HumanGen数据集（74.2K配对，8.6K任务）；(2) 引入上下文未来块预测（IFP）训练目标，抑制从已见任务中学到的捷径，迫使策略从视频提示中提取任务信息。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0仿真环境的七个未见任务上，Zero-WAM达到47.0%平均成功率，相较于最强视频-动作基线绝对提升29.5个百分点。在真实世界评估中，能够跟随人类视频引导泛化到未见任务配置，包括多物体场景、长时程操作和精细插入任务。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了以人类视频作为任务规范实现机器人上下文学习泛化的可行性。通过大规模数据构建和专门的训练目标设计，Zero-WAM展示了在仿真和真实环境中跨任务泛化的能力，为机器人操作领域提供了不依赖语言的任务指定新范式。

Abstract: Zero-shot cross-task generalization, where a policy must execute manipulation tasks never seen during training, remains a central challenge in robot learning. In large language models, a novel task can be performed simply by specifying it in the context, without any parameter update. This form of in-context learning (ICL) turns generalization into a problem of task specification. To achieve cross-task generalization, we bring this paradigm to robotic manipulation, and argue that the natural task specification for manipulation is a human video: unlike language, it provides rich visual cues about the intended task evolution. We present Zero-WAM, a causal video-action model that executes unseen tasks by following in-context human video guidance. To address the scarcity of task-rich paired human-robot data, we propose an automatic pipeline that converts task-sampled robot trajectories into semantically matched human videos, yielding HumanGen, a dataset of 74.2K human-robot ICL pairs across 8.6K tasks. For model training, we further introduce an in-context future chunk prediction (IFP) objective that suppresses shortcuts learned from seen tasks and forces the policy to draw task information from the video prompt. On seven unseen tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 simulation, Zero-WAM achieves a 47.0% average success rate, an absolute improvement of 29.5 percentage points over the strongest video-action baseline. In real-world evaluations, it follows human video guidance to generalize to unseen task configurations involving multi-object scenes, long-horizon manipulation, and fine-grained insertion.
